Index of **all 2,335 public uploads** on the [freeCodeCamp.org channel](https://www.youtube.com/@freecodecamp) as of 2026-10-14. This includes courses, short clips, and live streams. Publication ages are calculated from that capture date; the calendar dates stay useful as this page ages.

The first table brings SQL, PostgreSQL, Kubernetes, AWS, and system design to the top. Topic tags come from title keywords, so a project or discussion may appear beside a dedicated course. Both tables are sorted newest first.

Related notes: [[SQL]] · [[PostgreSQL]] · [[System Design]]

## Suggested starting points

- [Design Software Systems Like a Senior Engineer – Complete Course](https://youtu.be/soTfFFIQQ8U) for system design.
- [Harvard CS50’s Intro to Databases with SQL](https://youtu.be/WXk7yDqsKxs) for SQL, then [PostgreSQL Tutorial for Beginners](https://youtu.be/SpfIwlAYaKk).
- [Learn Kubernetes in 6 Hours](https://youtu.be/_4uQI4ihGVU) before the [2026 CKA preparation course](https://youtu.be/l57xKN6OBhY).
- [AWS Certified Cloud Practitioner 2026 course](https://youtu.be/7HKot-brXFE) for an AWS starting point.
